Model before rewriting:

letting b be 3
letting k be 3
letting l be 20
letting r be 60
letting v be 3
find bibd: matrix indexed by [int(1..v), int(1..b)] of int(0..1)
find x: int(-5..5)
find y: int(-5..5)

such that

bibd[bibd[x, y], 0] = 1

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
bibd

  or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))

  (x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [-5, 5]) >= -5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [-5, 5]) <= 5;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
